    function updatePagePositionUi() {
        const total = state.paginationResult?.pages?.length || 0;
        $$('.page-position-current').forEach(el => { el.textContent = total ? `${Math.max(1, Math.min(total, Number(state.currentPage) || 1))} / ${total}` : '—'; });
        $$('.page-nav-item').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('active', Number(el.dataset.page) === Number(state.currentPage)));
    }
    function jumpToPage(pageNumber, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const total = state.paginationResult?.pages?.length || 0;
        const requested = Math.round(Number(pageNumber) || 1);
        const targetIndex = Math.max(1, Math.min(total || 1, requested));
        const page = $(`#pages-wrap .page[data-page-index="${targetIndex}"]`);
        if (!page) return false;
        state.currentPage = targetIndex;
        page.scrollIntoView({ behavior, block: 'start', inline: 'center' });
        updatePagePositionUi();
        return true;
    }
    let pageScrollFrame = 0;
    function updateCurrentPageFromScroll() {
        if (state.previewMode !== 'pages') return;
        if (pageScrollFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(pageScrollFrame);
        pageScrollFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            pageScrollFrame = 0;
            const scroll = $('#preview-scroll');
            const pages = [...$$('#pages-wrap .page')];
            if (!scroll || !pages.length) return;
            const center = scroll.getBoundingClientRect().top + Math.min(scroll.clientHeight * .42, 320);
            let best = pages[0], distance = Infinity;
            for (const page of pages) {
                const rect = page.getBoundingClientRect();
                const d = Math.abs((rect.top + Math.min(rect.height * .18, 100)) - center);
                if (d < distance) { distance = d; best = page; }
            }
            const next = Number(best.dataset.pageIndex || 1);
            if (next !== state.currentPage) { state.currentPage = next; updatePagePositionUi(); }
        });
    }

    function registerGlobalShortcuts() {
        const scope = nativeAppShell.lifecycle.scope('global');
        scope.listen(document, 'keydown', async (e) => {
            const mod = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey, target = e.target, typing = target instanceof HTMLInputElement || target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement || target instanceof HTMLSelectElement || target?.isContentEditable, modalOpen = !!document.querySelector('.modal-layer');
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.modal) {
                closeModal();
                return;
            }
            if (e.key === 'F1' && !modalOpen) { e.preventDefault(); await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('help', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' }); return; }
            if (!mod)
                return;
            const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
            if (key === 'k' && !modalOpen) {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (state.screen === 'editor')
                    openCommandPalette();
                return;
            }
            if (key === '/' && !modalOpen) {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('keyboard-shortcuts', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (key === 's') {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('save-local', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (e.shiftKey && key === 'e' && !modalOpen) {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('export', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (key === 'n' && !modalOpen) {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('new', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (key === 'o' && !modalOpen) {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeApplication('open-file', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (e.key === 'Enter' && state.screen === 'editor' && !modalOpen && target?.closest?.('[data-editor-engine]')) {
                e.preventDefault();
                await nativeAppShell.commands.executeEditor('pagebreak', { element: target, source: 'keyboard' });
                return;
            }
            if (typing)
                return;
        });
    }

    function renderHome() {
        const libraryBytes = state.projects.reduce((sum, p) => sum + bytes(JSON.stringify(p)), 0), health = state.storageHealth;
        const usage = health?.usage ?? libraryBytes, quota = health?.quota ?? 50 * 1024 * 1024, storagePct = Math.min(100, quota > 0 ? usage / quota * 100 : 0);
        const libraryUnavailable = state.libraryStatus?.available === false;
        const cards = libraryUnavailable
            ? `<div class="diag-item error" style="grid-column:1/-1"><div class="diag-title">Project library unavailable</div><div class="diag-desc">${escapeHtml(state.libraryStatus?.error || 'Manuscript could not read browser storage.')} Your stored projects have not been interpreted as an empty library. Retry or open Storage Health before making destructive changes.</div></div>${state.projects.map(p => recentCard(p)).join('')}`
            : state.projects.map(p => recentCard(p)).join('') || `<div class="panel-empty" style="grid-column:1/-1"><h3>Create your first document</h3><p>Start blank, use a template, or open an existing Markdown file.</p></div>`;
        const driver = health?.driver === 'indexeddb' ? 'IndexedDB · split storage' : health?.driver === 'localstorage' ? 'Limited localStorage fallback' : 'Session-only memory';
        const persistent = health?.sessionOnly ? 'Backup required' : health?.persistent === true ? 'Persistent' : health?.persistent === false ? 'Best effort' : health?.durable === true ? 'Durable this browser' : 'Persistence unknown';
        const issue = health?.issues?.[0] ? `<div class="search-context" style="color:var(--warning)">${escapeHtml(health.issues[0])}</div>` : '';
        return `<main class="home-screen"><nav class="home-nav"><div class="brand"><span class="brand-mark">M</span><div><div class="brand-name">Manuscript</div><div class="brand-sub">Markdown Publishing Studio</div></div></div><div class="appbar-spacer"></div><button class="icon-btn" data-action="theme" aria-label="Choose interface theme" aria-haspopup="dialog">${icon(currentInterfaceTheme().dark ?     function renderApp() {
        disposeEditor();
        state.shellRevision++;
        nativeAppShell.store.touch('screen-render');
        applyInterfaceTheme();
        document.documentElement.dataset.density = state.interfaceDensity;
        document.documentElement.dataset.screen = state.screen;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => globalThis.__MANUSCRIPT_VISUAL_VIEWPORT_HANDLER__?.());
        const app = $('#app');
        if (state.screen === 'landing')
            app.innerHTML = renderLanding();
        else if (state.screen === 'home')
            app.innerHTML = renderHome();
        else
            app.innerHTML = renderEditor();
        app.className = '';
        bindGlobalEvents();
        if (state.screen === 'editor') {
            setupEditor();
            updateRender('layout');
        }
        renderModal();
        renderToasts();
        applyGeneratedLabels(app);
    }
